HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_099065224fc8083d8eef","text":"91:9-10 showed that inward uptake does not determine one outcome; it equips action in either direction. The sharper later surprise came from 91:12-13: forceful setting-out followed by a she-camel's water entitlement made `و ق ي/B003` legible as guarded movement around a vulnerable boundary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001677/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1a095323b02d8c759802","text":"1. **Two opposed boundary mechanics.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ف ج ر/B001` is a wide split and outflow and `ف ج ر/B004` is a breach of restraint; fixed `و ق ي/B001` is a barrier that wards harm and `و ق ي/B002` is placing the self in protective caution. **Structural cue and role:** `فجورها` and `تقواها` are coordinated as contents of one formed self, so the first functions as rupture and the second as boundary maintenance. **Abductive move:** the opposed branch images and syntax are supplied; modeling moral alternatives as permeability control is inferred. **Reading change:** the pair becomes an operational contrast between breaking a limit and maintaining a guard, not merely two abstract labels.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001381/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ad5744211fc264df6c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:8:1\",\"root_001381\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İlham, kötülük ile sakınma bilgisinin cana yalnızca bildirilmesi değil, onun içine bütünüyle alınmasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001035/B003","root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4ad43c297c8ba1f22d7e","text":"3. **Taqwa as guarded gait after pain.** Lexical evidence: و ق ي B003 \"guarded gait from hoof pain\"; ع ق ر B002 \"cutting an animal down\" and B003 \"disabling back or movement\" in 91:14; ن و ق B002 she-camel in 91:13. Structural cue: the later narrative centers on a she-camel being hamstrung. Assigned role: the hoof-pain branch gives taqwa a bodily caution around vulnerable movement. Abductive move: I infer retroactively that proper guarding includes not violating another creature's protected movement; the packet supplies hoof-guarding, she-camel, and hamstringing. Reading change: تقواها is not abstract religiosity only; it becomes careful movement around the vulnerable body and its rights.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4db986dbbffe26e2a092","text":"91:13–14 made تقوى more concrete than abstract piety: the protected she-camel and her watering right activate و ق ي as guarding a vulnerable boundary in the material world. The hoof-pain branch of و ق ي, which first seemed marginal, became surprisingly apt once عقر appeared as disabling the animal's movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002","root_001132/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5b30ab25955a8bf068ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:8:2\",\"root_001132\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kötülüğün şafak gibi belirmesi, sınır ihlalinin ilk anda aydınlanma ya da özgürleşme gibi görünebileceği yanıltıcı bir açıklık ihtimalini doğurur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B004","root_001381/B001","root_001677/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_62d442638c556ecf2533","text":"1. **Inspiration as complete intake of two moral disclosures.** Lexical evidence: ل ه م B001 \"swallowing and taking something in completely\"; ف ج ر B004 \"departure from right and breach of restraint\"; و ق ي B002 \"placing the self in protective caution.\" Structural cue: the verb ألهمها takes the soul as object, then gives two paired possessives: its فجور and its تقوى. Assigned role: ilham is ingestion/internalization; fujur and taqwa are the two knowable inner directions. Abductive move: I infer that the soul receives not merely data but an internal capacity to recognize both breach and guard; the packet supplies intake, breach, and caution branches. Reading change: the ayah reads less like external command and more like implanted discernment swallowed into the self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_67e08eb5dcf7871f82cc","text":"4. **`تقوى` as a guarded gait across bounded terrain.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `و ق ي/B003` is the cautious gait of an animal protecting a hurt hoof; contextual 91:6 spreads the earth, 91:12 has a forceful setting-out, and 91:13 names a she-camel and her water allotment. **Structural cue and role:** locomotion and a vulnerable resource boundary surround the fixed ayah, so caution functions as measured movement that avoids injuring what must be protected. **Abductive move:** the movement images are packet-given; applying the animal gait materially to moral conduct is analogical. **Reading change:** `تقواها` becomes practiced restraint in how one advances through shared space, not fear held only inside.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B003","root_001132/B001","root_001559/B003","root_001677/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_83fa848184d5065dd1c1","text":"2. **Fujur as split/outflow, taqwa as barrier/containment.** Lexical evidence: ف ج ر B001 \"wide splitting and outflow\"; و ق ي B001 \"warding harm off with a protective barrier\"; ن ه ر B003 opening/widening until flow in 91:3; س ق ي B003 irrigation channel in 91:13. Structural cue: the two nouns stand as opposites after the fashioned self. Assigned role: فجور is uncontrolled breach-flow; تقوى is protective containment. Abductive move: I supply the hydraulic moral model: water-like force must be channeled rather than burst out destructively; the packet supplies split/outflow, barrier, river opening, and watering. Reading change: the moral pair becomes material: sin is rupture of bounds, piety is guarding the channel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001677/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_af7db4955bb86da7aee0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:8:3\",\"root_001677\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sakınma, incinmiş bir ayağı koruyan dikkatli yürüyüş gibi, ileride devenin hareketine ve su hakkına zarar vermeden ortak alanda ilerleme biçimine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c853e5156054975ca8a9","text":"4. **Fujur as false dawn versus true dawn.** Lexical evidence: ف ج ر B002 \"dawn breaking from night, true and false dawn distinction\"; ك ذ ب B001 falsehood and B002 declaring false in 91:11/14; ج ل و B001 manifestation. Structural cue: the packet pairs revelation/unveiling with later denial. Assigned role: fujur's dawn branch introduces an ambiguous light that can mislead if not guarded. Abductive move: I infer that the soul must distinguish real disclosure from deceptive appearance; the packet supplies dawn distinction and deception/denial branches. Reading change: فجورها can include not only immoral overflow but a false-light epistemology that prepares the people's تكذيب.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001381/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d009be20e335a90dcdec","text":"2. **Inspiration as complete inward uptake.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ل ه م/B001` is swallowing or taking something in completely. **Structural cue and role:** the causative form `ألهم` acts on the self immediately after its formation and takes two explicit objects through the pronominal construction; the swallowing image gives the self the role of an inward recipient. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the root image and causative grammar; reversing ordinary ingestion into \"a source causes the self to internalize\" is the inferred directional arrow. **Reading change:** inspiration reads as embodied incorporation of moral knowledge rather than information merely placed nearby.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e5854c07efd2f9379b89","text":"3. **`فجور` can arrive with the phenomenology of dawn.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ف ج ر/B002` is dawn breaking from night, while `ف ج ر/B004` is moral breach. **Structural cue and role:** 91:3-4 established manifestation against covering, and `فجورها` now carries both an opening-light image and a transgressive one. **Abductive move:** coexistence of accepted branches is supplied; inferring that a breach may first present itself as disclosure or release is exploratory and does not erase the wickedness branch. **Reading change:** awareness of `فجور` includes the danger that boundary-breaking can feel like illumination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001132/B004","root_001677/B001","root_001677/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ff7f9daa736a8fd3c879","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:8:2\",\"root_001132\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"91:8:3\",\"root_001677\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kötülük sınırı yarıp taşan bir kuvvet, sakınma ise zararı durduran ve sınırı koruyan bir engel gibi karşı karşıya gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
